[\[286b.3\]]pa grub pa zhes bya ba/_nub phyogs pa rta mkhan bgrod dkas bsrungs pa'i bu nyi mas bsrungs pas byas pa rdzogs so/_/rgya gar gyi mkhan po dzi na mi tra dang /_a ti rgya warma dang /_lo tsA ba ban+de tsan+d+ras bsgyur te gtan la phab pa'o/_/bdag gis 
[\[286b.4\]]rig mchog gces grub 'di bris pas/_/rlung mkhris bad kan nad gzhi dug gsum dang /_/'phral gyi rkyen gyur nyon mongs gsum brgyad kyis/_/mnar med sems can mtha' yas thams cad kun/_/sman rnams bdud rtsir gyur nas nyam thag 
[\[286b.5\]]pa'i/_/rlung mkhris dug gsum la sogs gtan spangs te/_/sa rab bgrod dka' lam gyi nyes bsal nas/_/g.yung drung bla med chen po myur thob shog//_//[
